   138	impl IvcContext for Loan {
   139	    type Context = Context;
   140	    fn new(std_lib: ZkStdLib, ctx: &Context) -> Self {
   141	        assert_eq!(
   142	            ctx,
   143	            &fixed_context(),
   144	            "unsupported fixed specialization context"
   145	        );
   146	        Self {
   147	            std_lib,
   148	            ctx: ctx.clone(),
   149	        }
   150	    }
   151	    fn write_context<W: Write>(ctx: &Context, writer: &mut W) -> io::Result<()> {
   152	        if ctx != &fixed_context() {
   153	            return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, "wrong context"));
   154	        }
   155	        for limb in ctx.immutable.iter().flatten() {
   156	            writer.write_all(&limb.to_le_bytes())?;
   157	        }
   158	        Ok(())
   159	    }
   160	    fn read_context<R: Read>(reader: &mut R) -> io::Result<Context> {
   161	        let mut ctx = Context {
   162	            immutable: [[0; 4]; 4],
   163	        };
   164	        for limb in ctx.immutable.iter_mut().flatten() {
   165	            let mut bytes = [0; 8];
   166	            reader.read_exact(&mut bytes)?;
   167	            *limb = u64::from_le_bytes(bytes);
   168	        }
   169	        if ctx != fixed_context() {
   170	            return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, "wrong context"));
   171	        }
   172	        Ok(ctx)
   173	    }
   174	}
   175	impl IvcState for Loan {
   176	    type State = State;
   177	    type AssignedState = AssignedState;
   178	    fn genesis(ctx: &Context) -> State {
   179	        assert_eq!(ctx, &fixed_context());
   180	        fixed_state(0)
   181	    }
   182	    fn decider(ctx: &Context, state: &State) -> bool {
   183	        // All private full-data fields must match the unique phase-indexed row.
   184	        // This decider supplements, and never replaces, in-circuit equalities.
   185	        state_index(ctx, state).is_some()
   186	    }
   187	}
   188	// Circuit constants come directly from the unchanged generated episode table.
   189	// Host arithmetic checks are separate controls and are never the VK binding.
   190	fn table_limbs(index: usize) -> Vec<F> {
   191	    wire(&State {
   192	        phase: index as u64,
   193	        financial: episode::FINANCIAL[index],
   194	        digests: episode::DIGESTS[index],
   195	    })
   196	    .into_iter()
   197	    .map(F::from)
   198	    .collect()
   199	}
   200	impl Loan {
   201	    fn assign_full(
   202	        &self,
   203	        layouter: &mut impl Layouter<F>,
   204	        phase: Value<F>,
   205	        value: Value<Vec<F>>,
   206	    ) -> Result<AssignedState, Error> {
   207	        let phase: AssignedNative<F> = self.std_lib.assign(layouter, phase)?;
   208	        // Canonical field value 0, 1 or 2; never low-bit truncation.
   209	        self.std_lib
   210	            .assert_lower_than_fixed(layouter, &phase, &3u128.into())?;
   211	        let mut cells = Vec::with_capacity(LIMBS);
   212	        for i in 0..LIMBS {
   213	            let cell: AssignedNative<F> = self
   214	                .std_lib
   215	                .assign(layouter, value.as_ref().map(|v| v[i]))?;
   216	            self.std_lib
   217	                .assert_lower_than_fixed(layouter, &cell, &(1u128 << 64).into())?;
   218	            cells.push(cell);
   219	        }
   220	        Ok(AssignedState {
   221	            phase,
   222	            limbs: cells.try_into().expect("exactly 54 limbs"),
   223	        })
   224	    }
   225	}
   226	impl IvcIO for Loan {
   227	    fn assign(
   228	        &self,
   229	        layouter: &mut impl Layouter<F>,
   230	        value: Value<State>,
   231	    ) -> Result<AssignedState, Error> {
   232	        self.assign_full(
   233	            layouter,
   234	            value.as_ref().map(|state| F::from(state.phase)),
   235	            value
   236	                .as_ref()
   237	                .map(|state| wire(state).into_iter().map(F::from).collect()),
   238	        )
   239	    }
   240	    fn constrain_as_public_input(
   241	        &self,
   242	        layouter: &mut impl Layouter<F>,
   243	        state: &AssignedState,
   244	    ) -> Result<(), Error> {
   245	        // Only called on the complete constant-selected successor in IvcCircuit.
   246	        self.std_lib
   247	            .constrain_as_public_input(layouter, &state.phase)
   248	    }
   249	    fn as_public_input(
   250	        &self,
   251	        _: &mut impl Layouter<F>,
   252	        state: &AssignedState,
   253	    ) -> Result<Vec<AssignedNative<F>>, Error> {
   254	        // IvcCircuit has already constrained all 54 predecessor limbs through
   255	        // circuit_transition before this feeds the prior-proof/genesis checks.
   256	        Ok(vec![state.phase.clone()])
   257	    }
   258	    fn format_public_input(state: &State) -> Vec<F> {
   259	        // Partial encoding: reject noncanonical full State values. On the three
   260	        // admitted rows phase is injective. This host guard is NOT the circuit
   261	        // binding; circuit_transition emits that binding for every private limb.
   262	        assert!(
   263	            state_index(&fixed_context(), state).is_some(),
   264	            "noncanonical full row"
   265	        );
   266	        vec![F::from(state.phase)]
   267	    }
   268	}
   269	impl IvcTransition for Loan {
   270	    type Witness = ();
   271	    fn arch() -> ZkStdLibArch {
   272	        ZkStdLibArch {
   273	            nb_arith_cols: 9,
   274	            nr_pow2range_cols: 8,
   275	            ..ZkStdLibArch::default()
   276	        }
   277	    }
   278	    fn transition(ctx: &Context, state: &State, _: ()) -> State {
   279	        checked_transition(ctx, state).expect("invalid ordinary transition")
   280	    }
   281	    fn circuit_transition(
   282	        &self,
   283	        layouter: &mut impl Layouter<F>,
   284	        state: &AssignedState,
   285	        _: Value<()>,
   286	    ) -> Result<AssignedState, Error> {
   287	        assert_eq!(self.ctx, fixed_context());
   288	        // Ordinary predecessors are exactly phase 0 or 1. A phase-2 row must
   289	        // never bypass the genesis/prior-proof boundary as a fresh input.
   290	        self.std_lib
   291	            .assert_lower_than_fixed(layouter, &state.phase, &2u128.into())?;
   292	        let genesis: AssignedBit<F> =
   293	            self.std_lib
   294	                .is_equal_to_fixed(layouter, &state.phase, F::ZERO)?;
   295	        let before0 = table_limbs(0);
   296	        let before1 = table_limbs(1);
   297	        let after1 = table_limbs(2);
   298	        let mut next = Vec::with_capacity(LIMBS);
   299	        for i in 0..LIMBS {
   300	            let a: AssignedNative<F> = self.std_lib.assign_fixed(layouter, before0[i])?;
   301	            let b: AssignedNative<F> = self.std_lib.assign_fixed(layouter, before1[i])?;
   302	            let c: AssignedNative<F> = self.std_lib.assign_fixed(layouter, after1[i])?;
   303	            let expected = self.std_lib.select(layouter, &genesis, &a, &b)?;
   304	            self.std_lib
   305	                .assert_equal(layouter, &state.limbs[i], &expected)?;
   306	            let output = self.std_lib.select(layouter, &genesis, &b, &c)?;
   307	            self.std_lib
   308	                .assert_lower_than_fixed(layouter, &output, &(1u128 << 64).into())?;
   309	            next.push(output);
   310	        }
   311	        let next_phase = self.std_lib.add_constant(layouter, &state.phase, F::ONE)?;
   312	        self.std_lib
   313	            .assert_lower_than_fixed(layouter, &next_phase, &3u128.into())?;
   314	        Ok(AssignedState {
   315	            phase: next_phase,
   316	            limbs: next.try_into().expect("exactly 54 limbs"),
   317	        })
   318	    }
   319	}
   320	
   321	fn hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
   322	    bytes.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect()
   323	}
   324	fn sha(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
   325	    hex(&Sha256::digest(bytes))
   326	}
   327	fn event(name: &str, stage: &str, outcome: &str) {
   328	    println!(
   329	        "{{\"event\":\"control\",\"name\":\"{name}\",\"stage\":\"{stage}\",\"outcome\":\"{outcome}\"}}"
   330	    );
   331	}
   332	fn preflight_native() {
   333	    assert!(
   334	        F::NUM_BITS > 64,
   335	        "field cannot injectively encode u64 limbs"
   336	    );
   337	    assert_eq!(
   338	        native_financial(),
   339	        episode::FINANCIAL,
   340	        "independent calculation differs from R2 evaluator"
   341	    );
   342	    assert_eq!(episode::FIELD_NAMES.len(), 11);
   343	    assert_eq!(episode::DIGEST_NAMES.len(), 8);
   344	    for i in 0..3 {
   345	        assert_eq!(&episode::DIGESTS[i][..4], &episode::DIGESTS[0][..4]);
   346	        let s = fixed_state(i);
   347	        assert!(Loan::decider(&fixed_context(), &s));
   348	        assert_eq!(wire(&s).len(), LIMBS);
   349	        assert_eq!(LIMBS, 54);
   350	        assert_eq!(Loan::format_public_input(&s), vec![F::from(i as u64)]);
   351	        for phase in [0, 1, 2, 3, u64::MAX] {
   352	            let mut wrong_phase = s.clone();
   353	            wrong_phase.phase = phase;
   354	            assert_eq!(
   355	                Loan::decider(&fixed_context(), &wrong_phase),
   356	                phase == i as u64
   357	            );
   358	        }
   359	        for j in 0..episode::NUM_FIELDS {
   360	            for bit in [0, 64] {
   361	                let mut bad = s.clone();
   362	                bad.financial[j] ^= 1u128 << bit;
   363	                assert!(!Loan::decider(&fixed_context(), &bad));
   364	                assert!(checked_transition(&fixed_context(), &bad).is_err());
   365	                // Same phase is intentionally insufficient for an arbitrary
   366	                // host row; format_public_input rejects this invalid domain.
   367	                assert_eq!(s.phase, bad.phase);
   368	            }
   369	        }
   370	        for j in 0..episode::NUM_DIGESTS {
   371	            for limb in 0..4 {
   372	                let mut bad = s.clone();
   373	                bad.digests[j][limb] ^= 1;
   374	                assert!(!Loan::decider(&fixed_context(), &bad));
   375	                assert!(checked_transition(&fixed_context(), &bad).is_err());
   376	            }
   377	        }
   378	    }
   379	    for n in [0, u64::MAX as u128, 1u128 << 64, u128::MAX] {
   380	        let mut s = fixed_state(0);
   381	        s.financial[0] = n;
   382	        let encoded = wire(&s);
   383	        assert_eq!(encoded[0] as u128 | ((encoded[1] as u128) << 64), n);
   384	    }
   385	    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
   386	    Loan::write_context(&fixed_context(), &mut bytes).unwrap();
   387	    assert_eq!(
   388	        Loan::read_context(&mut Cursor::new(&bytes)).unwrap(),
   389	        fixed_context()
   390	    );
   391	    bytes[0] ^= 1;
   392	    assert!(Loan::read_context(&mut Cursor::new(&bytes)).is_err());
   393	    for i in 0..2 {
   394	        assert_eq!(
   395	            checked_transition(&fixed_context(), &fixed_state(i)).unwrap(),
   396	            fixed_state(i + 1)
   397	        );
   398	    }
   399	    assert!(checked_transition(&fixed_context(), &fixed_state(2)).is_err());
   400	    event(
   401	        "independent_arithmetic_all_state_limb_mutations_context_and_closed",
   402	        "native_host",
   403	        "pass",
   404	    );
   405	}
   406	
   407	// Exhaustive fixed-size application controls. They are executable preparation,
   408	// not executed evidence. All cases must finish inside the single runner budget.
   409	#[derive(Clone)]
   410	struct LocalRelation {
   411	    phase: F,
   412	    before: Vec<F>,
   413	    after_phase: F,
   414	    after: Vec<F>,
   415	    range_only: bool,
   416	}
   417	impl Relation for LocalRelation {
   418	    type Instance = ();
   419	    type Witness = ();
   420	    type Error = Error;
   421	    fn format_instance(_: &()) -> Result<Vec<F>, Error> {
   422	        Ok(vec![])
   423	    }
   424	    fn used_chips(&self) -> ZkStdLibArch {
   425	        Loan::arch()
   426	    }
   427	    fn circuit(
   428	        &self,
   429	        lib: &ZkStdLib,
   430	        layouter: &mut impl Layouter<F>,
   431	        _: Value<()>,
   432	        _: Value<()>,
   433	    ) -> Result<(), Error> {
   434	        let loan = Loan::new(lib.clone(), &fixed_context());
   435	        let before = loan.assign_full(
   436	            layouter,
   437	            Value::known(self.phase),
   438	            Value::known(self.before.clone()),
   439	        )?;
   440	        if !self.range_only {
   441	            let after = loan.circuit_transition(layouter, &before, Value::known(()))?;
   442	            lib.assert_equal_to_fixed(layouter, &after.phase, self.after_phase)?;
   443	            for (cell, constant) in after.limbs.iter().zip(&self.after) {
   444	                lib.assert_equal_to_fixed(layouter, cell, *constant)?;
   445	            }
   446	        }
   447	        Ok(())
   448	    }
   449	    fn write_relation<W: Write>(&self, _: &mut W) -> io::Result<()> {
   450	        Err(io::Error::other("local test relation is not serialized"))
   451	    }
   452	    fn read_relation<R: Read>(_: &mut R) -> io::Result<Self> {
   453	        Err(io::Error::other("local test relation is not serialized"))
   454	    }
   455	}
   456	fn local_case(name: &str, relation: LocalRelation, expected: bool) {
   457	    let circuit = MidnightCircuit::new(&relation, Value::known(()), Value::known(()), Some(K));
   458	    // Synthesis errors are not counted as successful rejection controls.
   459	    let prover = MockProver::run(&circuit, vec![vec![], vec![]]).expect("local synthesis failed");
   460	    let accepted = prover.verify().is_ok();
   461	    assert_eq!(accepted, expected, "unexpected local outcome: {name}");
   462	    event(
   463	        name,
   464	        "local_circuit",
   465	        if accepted { "accepted" } else { "rejected" },
   466	    );
   467	}
   468	fn base_case(phase: usize) -> LocalRelation {
   469	    LocalRelation {
   470	        phase: F::from(phase as u64),
   471	        before: table_limbs(phase),
   472	        after_phase: F::from((phase + 1) as u64),
   473	        after: table_limbs(phase + 1),
   474	        range_only: false,
   475	    }
   476	}
   477	fn preflight_circuit() {
   478	    for phase in 0..2 {
   479	        let base = base_case(phase);
   480	        local_case(&format!("valid_phase_{phase}"), base.clone(), true);
   481	        for limb in 0..LIMBS {
   482	            let mut before = base.clone();
   483	            before.before[limb] += F::ONE;
   484	            local_case(&format!("before_phase_{phase}_limb_{limb}"), before, false);
   485	            let mut after = base.clone();
   486	            after.after[limb] += F::ONE;
   487	            local_case(&format!("after_phase_{phase}_limb_{limb}"), after, false);
   488	        }
   489	        for row in 0..3 {
   490	            let mut wrong_before = base.clone();
   491	            wrong_before.before = table_limbs(row);
   492	            local_case(
   493	                &format!("phase_{phase}_before_row_{row}"),
   494	                wrong_before,
   495	                row == phase,
   496	            );
   497	            let mut wrong_after = base.clone();
   498	            wrong_after.after = table_limbs(row);
   499	            local_case(
   500	                &format!("phase_{phase}_after_row_{row}"),
   501	                wrong_after,
   502	                row == phase + 1,
   503	            );
   504	        }
   505	        for after_phase in [0u64, 1, 2, 3, u64::MAX] {
   506	            let mut wrong_edge = base.clone();
   507	            wrong_edge.after_phase = F::from(after_phase);
   508	            local_case(
   509	                &format!("edge_{phase}_to_{after_phase}"),
   510	                wrong_edge,
   511	                after_phase == phase as u64 + 1,
   512	            );
   513	        }
   514	    }
   515	    for (name, phase) in [
   516	        ("closed_phase", F::from(2)),
   517	        ("phase_three", F::from(3)),
   518	        ("phase_u64max", F::from(u64::MAX)),
   519	        ("phase_modular_minus_one", -F::ONE),
   520	    ] {
   521	        let mut bad = base_case(0);
   522	        bad.phase = phase;
   523	        if name == "closed_phase" {
   524	            bad.before = table_limbs(2);
   525	        }
   526	        local_case(name, bad, false);
   527	    }
   528	    // Raw-field tests bypass native u64 construction and exercise every limb.
   529	    for limb in 0..LIMBS {
   530	        let mut bad = base_case(0);
   531	        bad.range_only = true;
   532	        bad.before[limb] = F::from(u64::MAX) + F::ONE;
   533	        local_case(&format!("raw_limb_{limb}_2pow64"), bad, false);
   534	    }
   535	    let mut boundary = base_case(0);
   536	    boundary.range_only = true;
   537	    boundary.before = vec![F::from(u64::MAX); LIMBS];
   538	    local_case("all_u64_max_range_boundary", boundary, true);
   539	}
   540	
   541	fn main() {
   542	    assert!(
   543	        cfg!(feature = "truncated-challenges"),
   544	        "enable truncated-challenges"
   545	    );
   546	    let candidate_sha =
   547	        std::env::var("MORIARTY_R3_CANDIDATE_SHA256").expect("reviewed candidate hash required");
   548	    assert!(
   549	        candidate_sha.len() == 64
   550	            && candidate_sha
   551	                .bytes()
   552	                .all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&b))
   553	    );
   554	    let output = std::env::var("MORIARTY_R3_OUTPUT").expect("MORIARTY_R3_OUTPUT required");
   555	    let srs_path = std::env::var("MORIARTY_R3_SRS").expect("MORIARTY_R3_SRS required");
   556	    fs::create_dir_all(&output).unwrap();
   557	    let output = Path::new(&output);
   558	    println!(
   559	        "{{\"event\":\"start\",\"predicate\":\"{PREDICATE}\",\"k\":{K},\"steps\":2,\"episode_sha256\":\"{}\",\"source_commit\":\"{}\"}}",
   560	        episode::EPISODE_SHA256,
   561	        episode::INPUT_SOURCE_COMMIT
   562	    );
   563	    preflight_native();
   564	    preflight_circuit();
   565	    let degree = IvcCircuit::<Loan>::cs_degree();
   566	    let blowup =
   567	        <KZGCommitmentScheme<E> as PolynomialCommitmentScheme<F>>::srs_monomial_blowup(degree);
   568	    assert_eq!(blowup, 1, "only unextended K17 SRS authorized");
   569	    assert!(
   570	        fs::metadata(&srs_path).unwrap().len() <= 64 * 1024 * 1024,
   571	        "SRS exceeds bounded local input size"
   572	    );
   573	    let bytes = fs::read(&srs_path).expect("local SRS unavailable");
   574	    assert!(bytes.len() >= 4);
   575	    assert_eq!(
   576	        u32::from_le_bytes(bytes[..4].try_into().unwrap()),
   577	        K,
   578	        "SRS header must be K17 before decoding"
   579	    );
   580	    let srs_hash = sha(&bytes);
   581	    assert_eq!(
   582	        srs_hash, "4a9ef6c7c0619aab74eede44b13e753e3ba54508a02dd3b7106a949aabb73b74",
   583	        "unreviewed SRS bytes"
   584	    );
   585	    let mut cursor = Cursor::new(&bytes);
   586	    // Match the pinned Midnight trusted-setup file encoding. Trust is in the
   587	    // parent's pinned hash receipt; RawBytesUnchecked is not a ceremony audit.
   588	    let srs = ParamsKZG::<E>::read_custom(&mut cursor, SerdeFormat::RawBytesUnchecked)
   589	        .expect("SRS decoding failed");
   590	    assert_eq!(
   591	        cursor.position() as usize,
   592	        bytes.len(),
   593	        "trailing SRS bytes"
   594	    );
   595	    assert_eq!(srs.max_k(), K);
   596	    assert_eq!(srs.g_monomial_size(), 1usize << K);
   597	    println!(
   598	        "{{\"event\":\"srs_loaded\",\"sha256\":\"{srs_hash}\",\"bytes\":{},\"cs_degree\":{degree},\"monomial_blowup\":{blowup},\"format\":\"RawBytesUnchecked\"}}",
   599	        bytes.len()
   600	    );
   601	    drop(cursor);
   602	    drop(bytes);
   603	    let start = Instant::now();
   604	    println!("{{\"event\":\"setup_started\"}}");
   605	    let srs_verifier_params = srs.verifier_params();
   606	    let (mut prover, verifier) = ivc::setup::<Loan>(srs, K, fixed_context());
   607	    println!(
   608	        "{{\"event\":\"setup_complete\",\"milliseconds\":{}}}",
   609	        start.elapsed().as_millis()
   610	    );
   611	    let mut proofs = Vec::new();
   612	    let mut proof_hashes = Vec::new();
   613	    let mut sizes = Vec::new();
   614	    for step in 1..=2 {
   615	        let start = Instant::now();
   616	        let proof = prover.prove_step(()).expect("positive prove_step failed");
   617	        let instance = prover.instance();
   618	        assert_eq!(instance.state(), &fixed_state(step));
   619	        assert!(
   620	            proof.len() <= 32 * 1024 * 1024,
   621	            "proof exceeds artifact budget"
   622	        );
   623	        fs::write(output.join(format!("step-{step}.proof")), &proof).unwrap();
   624	        let pi = IvcCircuit::<Loan>::format_instance(&instance).unwrap();
   625	        assert_eq!(
   626	            pi[1],
   627	            F::from(step as u64),
   628	            "application PI is canonical phase"
   629	        );
   630	        if step == 1 {
   631	            fs::write(
   632	                output.join("canonical-vk-repr.le.bin"),
   633	                pi[0].to_repr().as_ref(),
   634	            )
   635	            .unwrap();
   636	        } else {
   637	            assert_eq!(
   638	                fs::read(output.join("canonical-vk-repr.le.bin")).unwrap(),
   639	                pi[0].to_repr().as_ref()
   640	            );
   641	        }
   642	        let pi_bytes: Vec<u8> = pi
   643	            .iter()
   644	            .flat_map(|f| f.to_repr().as_ref().to_vec())
   645	            .collect();
   646	        assert!(
   647	            pi_bytes.len() <= 1024 * 1024,
   648	            "public inputs exceed artifact budget"
   649	        );
   650	        fs::write(
   651	            output.join(format!("step-{step}.public-inputs.le.bin")),
   652	            &pi_bytes,
   653	        )
   654	        .unwrap();
   655	        fs::write(
   656	            output.join(format!("step-{step}.state-limbs.le.bin")),
   657	            wire(instance.state())
   658	                .iter()
   659	                .flat_map(|v| v.to_le_bytes())
   660	                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
   661	        )
   662	        .unwrap();
   663	        // Preserve bytes/PI before verification, including a failed result.
   664	        // Native verifier discharges BOTH the proof and carried accumulator.
   665	        verifier
   666	            .verify(&instance, &proof)
   667	            .expect("positive final-accumulator verification failed");
   668	        sizes.push(proof.len());
   669	        proof_hashes.push(sha(&proof));
   670	        println!(
   671	            "{{\"event\":\"step_verified\",\"step\":{step},\"proof_bytes\":{},\"proof_sha256\":\"{}\",\"public_input_elements\":{},\"public_inputs_sha256\":\"{}\",\"milliseconds\":{}}}",
   672	            proof.len(),
   673	            sha(&proof),
   674	            pi.len(),
   675	            sha(&pi_bytes),
   676	            start.elapsed().as_millis()
   677	        );
   678	        proofs.push(proof);
   679	    }
   680	    let final_instance = prover.instance();
   681	    let final_proof = &proofs[1];
   682	    let mut altered = final_proof.clone();
   683	    let midpoint = altered.len() / 2;
   684	    altered[midpoint] ^= 1;
   685	    let mut appended = final_proof.clone();
   686	    appended.push(0);
   687	    for (name, proof) in [
   688	        ("absent_proof", &[][..]),
   689	        ("truncated_proof", &final_proof[..final_proof.len() / 2]),
   690	        ("altered_proof", &altered),
   691	        ("appended_proof", &appended),
   692	        ("step1_proof_for_step2_statement", &proofs[0]),
   693	    ] {
   694	        assert!(
   695	            verifier.verify(&final_instance, proof).is_err(),
   696	            "unexpected acceptance: {name}"
   697	        );
   698	        event(name, "native_verifier", "rejected");
   699	    }
   700	    // resume_from is the only public state mutation interface. These are
   701	    // explicitly DECIDER controls: the replacement accumulator is irrelevant
   702	    // because verifier::verify checks the decider before touching it.
   703	    for (name, financial, digest) in [
   704	        ("altered_cash", Some(7), None),
   705	        ("altered_due", Some(4), None),
   706	        ("wrong_domain", None, Some(0)),
   707	        ("wrong_program", None, Some(1)),
   708	        ("wrong_specification", None, Some(2)),
   709	        ("wrong_intent", None, Some(3)),
   710	        ("wrong_output", None, Some(5)),
   711	        ("wrong_effects", None, Some(6)),
   712	        ("mismatched_predecessor", None, Some(4)),
   713	        ("excessive_authority_commitment", None, Some(7)),
   714	        ("forged_genesis", Some(0), None),
   715	    ] {
   716	        let mut bad = if name == "forged_genesis" {
   717	            fixed_state(0)
   718	        } else {
   719	            fixed_state(2)
   720	        };
   721	        if let Some(i) = financial {
   722	            bad.financial[i] ^= 1;
   723	        }
   724	        if let Some(i) = digest {
   725	            bad.digests[i][0] ^= 1;
   726	        }
   727	        prover.resume_from(bad, Vec::new(), Accumulator::<S>::trivial(&[]));
   728	        let rejection = verifier.verify(&prover.instance(), final_proof);
   729	        assert!(
   730	            matches!(rejection, Err(ivc::IvcError::DeciderFailed)),
   731	            "expected decider rejection: {name}"
   732	        );
   733	        event(name, "native_verifier_application_decider", "rejected");
   734	    }
   735	    event(
   736	        "different_verifying_key",
   737	        "not_exercised",
   738	        "gap_private_vk_no_second_setup",
   739	    );
   740	    // An invalid carried accumulator with the same public shape, built through
   741	    // public APIs. Direct pairing failure and final-verifier rejection are distinct
   742	    // controls; this does not claim a newly proved invalid-accumulator chain.
   743	    let mut cs = ConstraintSystem::default();
   744	    ZkStdLib::configure(&mut cs, (IvcCircuit::<Loan>::arch(), (K - 1) as u8));
   745	    let labels = midnight_circuits::verifier::fixed_base_labels::<S>(
   746	        cs.num_fixed_columns() + cs.num_selectors(),
   747	        cs.permutation().columns.len(),
   748	    );
   749	    let trivial = Accumulator::<S>::trivial(&labels);
   750	    let invalid_lhs = Msm::<S>::new(
   751	        &[Point::Variable(<S as SelfEmulation>::C::generator())],
   752	        &[F::ONE],
   753	        &[PolynomialLabel::NoLabel],
   754	    );
   755	    let invalid_acc = Accumulator::<S>::new(invalid_lhs, trivial.rhs());
   756	    // All fixed-base coefficients in this RHS are zero; identity bases suffice
   757	    // for this direct invariant test and are not a substitute VK for verification.
   758	    let zero_rhs_bases = labels
   759	        .iter()
   760	        .cloned()
   761	        .map(|label| (label, <S as SelfEmulation>::C::identity()))
   762	        .collect();
   763	    assert!(
   764	        !invalid_acc.check(&srs_verifier_params, &zero_rhs_bases),
   765	        "invalid accumulator passed pairing"
   766	    );
   767	    prover.resume_from(fixed_state(2), final_proof.clone(), invalid_acc);
   768	    assert!(
   769	        matches!(
   770	            verifier.verify(&prover.instance(), final_proof),
   771	            Err(ivc::IvcError::InvalidProof)
   772	        ),
   773	        "invalid carried accumulator was not rejected after a valid application decider"
   774	    );
   775	    event(
   776	        "invalid_accumulator_pairing_and_final_statement",
   777	        "native_verifier",
   778	        "rejected",
   779	    );
   780	    event(
   781	        "verifier_serialization",
   782	        "not_supported",
   783	        "gap_no_public_serialization_api",
   784	    );
   785	    let receipt = format!(
   786	        "{{\"status\":\"completed_with_explicit_gaps\",\"candidate_sha256\":\"{candidate_sha}\",\"predicate\":\"{PREDICATE}\",\"fixed_instance_specialization\":true,\"steps_proved_and_verified\":2,\"final_accumulator_discharged\":true,\"application_public_elements\":1,\"private_application_elements\":55,\"proof_sizes\":[{},{}],\"proof_sha256\":[\"{}\",\"{}\"],\"episode_sha256\":\"{}\",\"srs_sha256\":\"{srs_hash}\",\"gaps\":[\"different_vk_control\",\"verifier_serialization\",\"dynamic_authorization\",\"general_refinement\",\"ledger_acceptance\"]}}\n",
   787	        sizes[0],
   788	        sizes[1],
   789	        proof_hashes[0],
   790	        proof_hashes[1],
   791	        episode::EPISODE_SHA256
   792	    );
   793	    fs::write(output.join("native-receipt.json"), &receipt).unwrap();
   794	    print!("{receipt}");
   795	}

    41	def read_regular(path, maximum=64 * 1024**2):
    42	    path = Path(path)
    43	    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size > maximum:
    44	        raise RuntimeError(f'Not a bounded regular input: {path}')
    45	    with path.open('rb') as stream:
    46	        data = stream.read(maximum + 1)
    47	    if len(data) > maximum:
    48	        raise RuntimeError('Input changed beyond byte bound')
    49	    return data

    52	def receipt(path, digest):

   105	def enforced_limits():

   112	        or values['memory.oom.group'] != '1' or quota == 'max'

   114	        raise RuntimeError('Required group limits absent')

   118	def checked_copy(source, destination, digest, maximum=64 * 1024**2):
   119	    data = read_regular(source, maximum)
   120	    if sha(data) != digest:
   121	        raise RuntimeError('Input hash mismatch: ' + str(source))
   122	    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   123	    with destination.open('xb') as stream:
   124	        stream.write(data)
   125	    destination.chmod(0o444)
   126	    return len(data)

   456	fn local_case(name: &str, relation: LocalRelation, expected: bool) {
   457	    let circuit = MidnightCircuit::new(&relation, Value::known(()), Value::known(()), Some(K));
   458	    // Synthesis errors are not counted as successful rejection controls.
   459	    let prover = MockProver::run(&circuit, vec![vec![], vec![]]).expect("local synthesis failed");
   460	    let accepted = prover.verify().is_ok();
   461	    assert_eq!(accepted, expected, "unexpected local outcome: {name}");
   462	    event(
   463	        name,
   464	        "local_circuit",
   465	        if accepted { "accepted" } else { "rejected" },
   466	    );
   467	}
   468	fn base_case(phase: usize) -> LocalRelation {
   469	    LocalRelation {
   470	        phase: F::from(phase as u64),
   471	        before: table_limbs(phase),
   472	        after_phase: F::from((phase + 1) as u64),
   473	        after: table_limbs(phase + 1),
   474	        range_only: false,
   475	    }
   476	}
   477	fn preflight_circuit() {
   478	    for phase in 0..2 {
   479	        let base = base_case(phase);
   480	        local_case(&format!("valid_phase_{phase}"), base.clone(), true);
   481	        for limb in 0..LIMBS {
   482	            let mut before = base.clone();
   483	            before.before[limb] += F::ONE;
   484	            local_case(&format!("before_phase_{phase}_limb_{limb}"), before, false);
   485	            let mut after = base.clone();
   486	            after.after[limb] += F::ONE;
   487	            local_case(&format!("after_phase_{phase}_limb_{limb}"), after, false);
   488	        }
   489	        for row in 0..3 {
   490	            let mut wrong_before = base.clone();
   491	            wrong_before.before = table_limbs(row);
   492	            local_case(
   493	                &format!("phase_{phase}_before_row_{row}"),
   494	                wrong_before,
   495	                row == phase,
   496	            );
   497	            let mut wrong_after = base.clone();
   498	            wrong_after.after = table_limbs(row);
   499	            local_case(
   500	                &format!("phase_{phase}_after_row_{row}"),
   501	                wrong_after,
   502	                row == phase + 1,
   503	            );
   504	        }
   505	        for after_phase in [0u64, 1, 2, 3, u64::MAX] {
   506	            let mut wrong_edge = base.clone();
   507	            wrong_edge.after_phase = F::from(after_phase);
   508	            local_case(
   509	                &format!("edge_{phase}_to_{after_phase}"),
   510	                wrong_edge,
   511	                after_phase == phase as u64 + 1,
   512	            );
   513	        }
   514	    }
   515	    for (name, phase) in [
   516	        ("closed_phase", F::from(2)),
   517	        ("phase_three", F::from(3)),
   518	        ("phase_u64max", F::from(u64::MAX)),
   519	        ("phase_modular_minus_one", -F::ONE),
   520	    ] {
   521	        let mut bad = base_case(0);
   522	        bad.phase = phase;
   523	        if name == "closed_phase" {
   524	            bad.before = table_limbs(2);
   525	        }
   526	        local_case(name, bad, false);
   527	    }
   528	    // Raw-field tests bypass native u64 construction and exercise every limb.
   529	    for limb in 0..LIMBS {
   530	        let mut bad = base_case(0);
   531	        bad.range_only = true;
   532	        bad.before[limb] = F::from(u64::MAX) + F::ONE;
   533	        local_case(&format!("raw_limb_{limb}_2pow64"), bad, false);
   534	    }
   535	    let mut boundary = base_case(0);
   536	    boundary.range_only = true;
   537	    boundary.before = vec![F::from(u64::MAX); LIMBS];
   538	    local_case("all_u64_max_range_boundary", boundary, true);
   539	}
   540	
   541	fn main() {
   542	    assert!(
   543	        cfg!(feature = "truncated-challenges"),
   544	        "enable truncated-challenges"
   545	    );
   546	    let candidate_sha =
   547	        std::env::var("MORIARTY_R3_CANDIDATE_SHA256").expect("reviewed candidate hash required");
   548	    assert!(
   549	        candidate_sha.len() == 64
   550	            && candidate_sha
   551	                .bytes()
   552	                .all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&b))
   553	    );
   554	    let output = std::env::var("MORIARTY_R3_OUTPUT").expect("MORIARTY_R3_OUTPUT required");
   555	    let srs_path = std::env::var("MORIARTY_R3_SRS").expect("MORIARTY_R3_SRS required");
   556	    fs::create_dir_all(&output).unwrap();
   557	    let output = Path::new(&output);
   558	    println!(
   559	        "{{\"event\":\"start\",\"predicate\":\"{PREDICATE}\",\"k\":{K},\"steps\":2,\"episode_sha256\":\"{}\",\"source_commit\":\"{}\"}}",
   560	        episode::EPISODE_SHA256,
   561	        episode::INPUT_SOURCE_COMMIT
   562	    );
   563	    preflight_native();
   564	    preflight_circuit();
   565	    let degree = IvcCircuit::<Loan>::cs_degree();
   566	    let blowup =
   567	        <KZGCommitmentScheme<E> as PolynomialCommitmentScheme<F>>::srs_monomial_blowup(degree);
   568	    assert_eq!(blowup, 1, "only unextended K17 SRS authorized");
   569	    assert!(
   570	        fs::metadata(&srs_path).unwrap().len() <= 64 * 1024 * 1024,
   571	        "SRS exceeds bounded local input size"
   572	    );
   573	    let bytes = fs::read(&srs_path).expect("local SRS unavailable");
   574	    assert!(bytes.len() >= 4);
   575	    assert_eq!(
   576	        u32::from_le_bytes(bytes[..4].try_into().unwrap()),
   577	        K,
   578	        "SRS header must be K17 before decoding"
   579	    );
   580	    let srs_hash = sha(&bytes);
   581	    assert_eq!(
   582	        srs_hash, "4a9ef6c7c0619aab74eede44b13e753e3ba54508a02dd3b7106a949aabb73b74",
   583	        "unreviewed SRS bytes"
   584	    );
   585	    let mut cursor = Cursor::new(&bytes);
   586	    // Match the pinned Midnight trusted-setup file encoding. Trust is in the
   587	    // parent's pinned hash receipt; RawBytesUnchecked is not a ceremony audit.
   588	    let srs = ParamsKZG::<E>::read_custom(&mut cursor, SerdeFormat::RawBytesUnchecked)
   589	        .expect("SRS decoding failed");
   590	    assert_eq!(
   591	        cursor.position() as usize,
   592	        bytes.len(),
   593	        "trailing SRS bytes"
   594	    );
   595	    assert_eq!(srs.max_k(), K);
   596	    assert_eq!(srs.g_monomial_size(), 1usize << K);
   597	    println!(
   598	        "{{\"event\":\"srs_loaded\",\"sha256\":\"{srs_hash}\",\"bytes\":{},\"cs_degree\":{degree},\"monomial_blowup\":{blowup},\"format\":\"RawBytesUnchecked\"}}",
   599	        bytes.len()
   600	    );
   601	    drop(cursor);
   602	    drop(bytes);
   603	    let start = Instant::now();
   604	    println!("{{\"event\":\"setup_started\"}}");
   605	    let srs_verifier_params = srs.verifier_params();
   606	    let (mut prover, verifier) = ivc::setup::<Loan>(srs, K, fixed_context());
   607	    println!(
   608	        "{{\"event\":\"setup_complete\",\"milliseconds\":{}}}",
   609	        start.elapsed().as_millis()
   610	    );
   611	    let mut proofs = Vec::new();
   612	    let mut proof_hashes = Vec::new();
   613	    let mut sizes = Vec::new();
   614	    for step in 1..=2 {
   615	        let start = Instant::now();
   616	        let proof = prover.prove_step(()).expect("positive prove_step failed");
   617	        let instance = prover.instance();
   618	        assert_eq!(instance.state(), &fixed_state(step));
   619	        assert!(
   620	            proof.len() <= 32 * 1024 * 1024,
   621	            "proof exceeds artifact budget"
   622	        );
   623	        fs::write(output.join(format!("step-{step}.proof")), &proof).unwrap();
   624	        let pi = IvcCircuit::<Loan>::format_instance(&instance).unwrap();
   625	        assert_eq!(
   626	            pi[1],
   627	            F::from(step as u64),
   628	            "application PI is canonical phase"
   629	        );
   630	        if step == 1 {
   631	            fs::write(
   632	                output.join("canonical-vk-repr.le.bin"),
   633	                pi[0].to_repr().as_ref(),
   634	            )
   635	            .unwrap();
   636	        } else {
   637	            assert_eq!(
   638	                fs::read(output.join("canonical-vk-repr.le.bin")).unwrap(),
   639	                pi[0].to_repr().as_ref()
   640	            );
   641	        }
   642	        let pi_bytes: Vec<u8> = pi
   643	            .iter()
   644	            .flat_map(|f| f.to_repr().as_ref().to_vec())
   645	            .collect();
   646	        assert!(
   647	            pi_bytes.len() <= 1024 * 1024,
   648	            "public inputs exceed artifact budget"
   649	        );
   650	        fs::write(
   651	            output.join(format!("step-{step}.public-inputs.le.bin")),
   652	            &pi_bytes,
   653	        )
   654	        .unwrap();
   655	        fs::write(
   656	            output.join(format!("step-{step}.state-limbs.le.bin")),
   657	            wire(instance.state())
   658	                .iter()
   659	                .flat_map(|v| v.to_le_bytes())
   660	                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
   661	        )
   662	        .unwrap();
   663	        // Preserve bytes/PI before verification, including a failed result.
   664	        // Native verifier discharges BOTH the proof and carried accumulator.
   665	        verifier
   666	            .verify(&instance, &proof)
   667	            .expect("positive final-accumulator verification failed");
   668	        sizes.push(proof.len());
   669	        proof_hashes.push(sha(&proof));
   670	        println!(
   671	            "{{\"event\":\"step_verified\",\"step\":{step},\"proof_bytes\":{},\"proof_sha256\":\"{}\",\"public_input_elements\":{},\"public_inputs_sha256\":\"{}\",\"milliseconds\":{}}}",
   672	            proof.len(),
   673	            sha(&proof),
   674	            pi.len(),
   675	            sha(&pi_bytes),
   676	            start.elapsed().as_millis()
   677	        );
   678	        proofs.push(proof);
   679	    }
   680	    let final_instance = prover.instance();
   681	    let final_proof = &proofs[1];
   682	    let mut altered = final_proof.clone();
   683	    let midpoint = altered.len() / 2;
   684	    altered[midpoint] ^= 1;
   685	    let mut appended = final_proof.clone();
   686	    appended.push(0);
   687	    for (name, proof) in [
   688	        ("absent_proof", &[][..]),
   689	        ("truncated_proof", &final_proof[..final_proof.len() / 2]),
   690	        ("altered_proof", &altered),
   691	        ("appended_proof", &appended),
   692	        ("step1_proof_for_step2_statement", &proofs[0]),
   693	    ] {
   694	        assert!(
   695	            verifier.verify(&final_instance, proof).is_err(),
   696	            "unexpected acceptance: {name}"
   697	        );
   698	        event(name, "native_verifier", "rejected");
   699	    }
   700	    // resume_from is the only public state mutation interface. These are
   701	    // explicitly DECIDER controls: the replacement accumulator is irrelevant
   702	    // because verifier::verify checks the decider before touching it.
   703	    for (name, financial, digest) in [
   704	        ("altered_cash", Some(7), None),
   705	        ("altered_due", Some(4), None),
   706	        ("wrong_domain", None, Some(0)),
   707	        ("wrong_program", None, Some(1)),
   708	        ("wrong_specification", None, Some(2)),
   709	        ("wrong_intent", None, Some(3)),
   710	        ("wrong_output", None, Some(5)),
   711	        ("wrong_effects", None, Some(6)),
   712	        ("mismatched_predecessor", None, Some(4)),
   713	        ("excessive_authority_commitment", None, Some(7)),
   714	        ("forged_genesis", Some(0), None),
   715	    ] {
   716	        let mut bad = if name == "forged_genesis" {
   717	            fixed_state(0)
   718	        } else {
   719	            fixed_state(2)
   720	        };
   721	        if let Some(i) = financial {
   722	            bad.financial[i] ^= 1;
   723	        }
   724	        if let Some(i) = digest {
   725	            bad.digests[i][0] ^= 1;
   726	        }
   727	        prover.resume_from(bad, Vec::new(), Accumulator::<S>::trivial(&[]));
   728	        let rejection = verifier.verify(&prover.instance(), final_proof);
   729	        assert!(
   730	            matches!(rejection, Err(ivc::IvcError::DeciderFailed)),
   731	            "expected decider rejection: {name}"
   732	        );
   733	        event(name, "native_verifier_application_decider", "rejected");
   734	    }
   735	    event(
   736	        "different_verifying_key",
   737	        "not_exercised",
   738	        "gap_private_vk_no_second_setup",
   739	    );
   740	    // An invalid carried accumulator with the same public shape, built through
   741	    // public APIs. Direct pairing failure and final-verifier rejection are distinct
   742	    // controls; this does not claim a newly proved invalid-accumulator chain.
   743	    let mut cs = ConstraintSystem::default();
   744	    ZkStdLib::configure(&mut cs, (IvcCircuit::<Loan>::arch(), (K - 1) as u8));
   745	    let labels = midnight_circuits::verifier::fixed_base_labels::<S>(
   746	        cs.num_fixed_columns() + cs.num_selectors(),
   747	        cs.permutation().columns.len(),
   748	    );
   749	    let trivial = Accumulator::<S>::trivial(&labels);
   750	    let invalid_lhs = Msm::<S>::new(
   751	        &[Point::Variable(<S as SelfEmulation>::C::generator())],
   752	        &[F::ONE],
   753	        &[PolynomialLabel::NoLabel],
   754	    );
   755	    let invalid_acc = Accumulator::<S>::new(invalid_lhs, trivial.rhs());
   756	    // All fixed-base coefficients in this RHS are zero; identity bases suffice
   757	    // for this direct invariant test and are not a substitute VK for verification.
   758	    let zero_rhs_bases = labels
   759	        .iter()
   760	        .cloned()
   761	        .map(|label| (label, <S as SelfEmulation>::C::identity()))
   762	        .collect();
   763	    assert!(
   764	        !invalid_acc.check(&srs_verifier_params, &zero_rhs_bases),
   765	        "invalid accumulator passed pairing"
   766	    );
   767	    prover.resume_from(fixed_state(2), final_proof.clone(), invalid_acc);
   768	    assert!(
   769	        matches!(
   770	            verifier.verify(&prover.instance(), final_proof),
   771	            Err(ivc::IvcError::InvalidProof)
   772	        ),
   773	        "invalid carried accumulator was not rejected after a valid application decider"
   774	    );
   775	    event(
   776	        "invalid_accumulator_pairing_and_final_statement",
   777	        "native_verifier",
   778	        "rejected",
   779	    );
   780	    event(
   781	        "verifier_serialization",
   782	        "not_supported",
   783	        "gap_no_public_serialization_api",
   784	    );
   785	    let receipt = format!(
   786	        "{{\"status\":\"completed_with_explicit_gaps\",\"candidate_sha256\":\"{candidate_sha}\",\"predicate\":\"{PREDICATE}\",\"fixed_instance_specialization\":true,\"steps_proved_and_verified\":2,\"final_accumulator_discharged\":true,\"application_public_elements\":1,\"private_application_elements\":55,\"proof_sizes\":[{},{}],\"proof_sha256\":[\"{}\",\"{}\"],\"episode_sha256\":\"{}\",\"srs_sha256\":\"{srs_hash}\",\"gaps\":[\"different_vk_control\",\"verifier_serialization\",\"dynamic_authorization\",\"general_refinement\",\"ledger_acceptance\"]}}\n",
   787	        sizes[0],
   788	        sizes[1],
   789	        proof_hashes[0],
   790	        proof_hashes[1],
   791	        episode::EPISODE_SHA256
   792	    );
   793	    fs::write(output.join("native-receipt.json"), &receipt).unwrap();
   794	    print!("{receipt}");
   795	}
